  const h = document.getElementById("siteheader");

  const isDesktopOnMobile =
    document.documentElement.classList.contains("desktop-on-mobile");

  const isSticky = h?.dataset.headerBehaviour === "sticky";
  const isTransparent = h?.dataset.headerLevel === "transparent";

  if (h && isSticky && isTransparent) {
    if (document.body.scrollTop >= 45 && !h.classList.contains("is-scrolled")) {
      h.classList.add("is-scrolled");
    }

    if (!isDesktopOnMobile) {
      window.addEventListener("scroll", () => {
        const scrollTop = document.documentElement.scrollTop;

        if (scrollTop >= 45 && !h.classList.contains("is-scrolled")) {
          h.classList.add("is-scrolled");
        } else if (scrollTop < 45) {
          h.classList.remove("is-scrolled");
        }
      });
    }

class="more-info"><h2 class="ff-b ts-24 ff-display c-accent mb-6"><span class="d-contents">Additional information</span></h2><div class="m-trim-block"><h3 class="bg-accent c-white br-small px-1 fs-11 ff-b mb-0 mt-6 w-fit"><span class="d-contents">General</span></h3><p><span class="d-contents">This is the wind, wave and weather forecast for Rockanje in South Holland, The Netherlands.</span><span class="d-contents">Windfinder specializes in wind, waves, tides and weather reports &amp; forecasts for wind related sports like kitesurfing, windsurfing, surfing, sailing, fishing or paragliding.</span></p><h3 class="bg-accent c-white br-small px-1 fs-11 ff-b mb-0 mt-6 w-fit"><span class="d-contents">Forecast</span></h3><p>This forecast is based on the GFS model. Forecasts are available worldwide. The horizontal resolution is about 13 km. Forecasts are computed 4 times a day, at about 01:00, 07:00, 13:00 and 19:00 Central European Summer Time. Predictions are available in time steps of 6 hours for up to 10 days into the future.<span class="d-contents">The arrows point in the direction in which the wind is blowing.</span></p><p><span class="d-contents">Check the wind forecast for Rockanje when you search for the best travel destinations for your kiteboarding, windsurfing or sailing vacations in The Netherlands.</span><span class="d-contents">Or use our wind forecast to find the wind speed today in Rockanje or to have a look at the wind direction tomorrow at Rockanje.</span></p><h3 class="bg-accent c-white br-small px-1 fs-11 ff-b mb-0 mt-6 w-fit"><span class="d-contents">Severe Weather Warnings</span></h3><p><span class="d-contents">When a severe weather warning or advisory is provided by the local meteorological institute, we display it as a banner above the wind forecast.</span></p><p>The weather warning, for example a high wind warning, can help you prepare for dangerous weather conditions and avoid weather-related risks. The color scale indicates the intensity of the predicted weather event. For more details click or tap on the warning. General information about severe weather warnings can be found in our <a href="/help/usage/severe-weather-warnings" class="ff-b c-accent js-lt-help_section">help section</a>.</p><p><span class="d-contents">Please be aware that severe weather may occur without a prior severe weather warning being reported.</span></p><h3 class="bg-accent c-white br-small px-1 fs-11 ff-b mb-0 mt-6 w-fit"><span class="d-contents">Units</span></h3><p>We use knots and degrees Celsius as our default units. These units are often used by sailors, kiters, surfers, windsurfers and paragliders. Use website settings to switch between units and 7 different languages at any time. For converting between wind speed units such as knots, km/h (kilometers per hour), m/s (meters per second), and mph (miles per hour) use our <a href="/wind/windspeed" class="ff-b c-accent js-lt-wind_speed_calculator">wind speed calculator</a>.</p><h3 class="bg-accent c-white br-small px-1 fs-11 ff-b mb-0 mt-6 w-fit"><span class="d-contents">Help</span></h3><p>If you need more information about our wind forecast for Rockanje, have a look at our <a href="/help/usage/forecast-superforecast" class="ff-b c-accent js-lt-help_section">help section</a>.</p></div></div></div><div
